We introduce R2D-RL, a reinforcement learning environment that connects RCSS2D and HELIOS-based player clients to a Python MARL interface through shared-memory communication and cycle-level synchronization. R2D-RL supports full-field and scenario-based training with configurable opponents, Base discrete and Hybrid parameterized action spaces, action masks, expected possession value (EPV)-based reward shaping, and parallel execution. We provide front-goal scenarios and an 11-vs-11 full-field benchmark, together with baseline results.

</details>


### [151] [ProfiLLM: Utility-Aligned Agentic User Profiling for Industrial Ride-Hailing Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18803)
*Tengfei Lyu,Zirui Yuan,Xu Liu,Kai Wan,Zihao Lu,Li Ma,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProfiLLM是一个面向工业级网约车调度系统的LLM数据管道，通过工具增强的全局知识挖掘和效用对齐的画像探索，实现可扩展、实用的用户行为画像，并在实际部署中显著提升匹配效率与业务指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调度系统依赖结构化数值特征，难以捕捉用户行为中的上下文信号（如司机区域偏好），而直接应用大语言模型面临上下文长度限制、长尾用户稀疏性以及生成内容与下游任务效用脱节的问题。

Method: 提出ProfiLLM，包含两个模块：(1) 工具增强的全局知识挖掘——赋予LLM代理27种分析工具，从平台级日志中提取可复用的全局知识、用户聚类规则和区域供需先验；(2) 效用对齐的画像探索——为每个用户簇生成多个候选画像，通过轻量级下游效用代理评估并迭代优化，生成用于DPO微调的偏好对。

Result: 在滴滴生产系统部署后，ProfiLLM在结果预测AUC上最高提升6.14%，派单模拟中GMV提升达4.35%；在线A/B测试显示GMV提升0.47%、完单率提升0.33%、拒接率降低0.82%。

Conclusion: ProfiLLM成功将大语言模型集成到高时效要求的工业调度系统中，通过代理式工具调用和效用驱动的画像构建，解决了大规模、长尾、低延迟场景下的语义特征提取难题，验证了LLM作为可操作语义引擎在现实世界系统中的可行性。

Abstract: Bringing Large Language Models (LLMs) into industrial ride-hailing dispatch as semantic feature extractors over platform-scale behavioral logs is a compelling but under-explored data systems problem. Production matching pipelines remain dominated by structured numerical features, yet decisive behavioral signals (e.g., a driver's habitual aversion to certain regions) are inherently contextual and naturally expressible as LLM-generated user profiles. However, scaling such profiling to a live, millisecond-latency dispatcher faces three intertwined constraints rarely addressed together: on a platform with millions of daily orders, logs exceed any LLM's context window by orders of magnitude; most users are long-tail, with too few interactions for per-user profiling; and surface-fluent profiles do not necessarily improve downstream prediction utility. We present ProfiLLM, an agentic LLM data pipeline that operationalizes utility-aligned user profiling for production matching systems through two modules. (1) Tool-Augmented Global Knowledge Mining equips an LLM agent with 27 analytical tools to mine platform-scale data, producing reusable global knowledge, adaptive user clustering rules, and region-level supply-demand priors. (2) Utility-Aligned Profile Exploration generates multiple candidate profiles per cluster, evaluates them via a lightweight downstream utility proxy, iteratively refines the best candidates and constructs preference pairs for DPO fine-tuning. Deployed on DiDi's production dispatcher, ProfiLLM achieves up to +6.14% relative AUC improvement in outcome prediction, up to +4.35% GMV gain in dispatching simulation, and consistent improvements in a 14-day online A/B test including +0.47% GMV, +0.33% Completion Rate, and -0.82% Cancel-Before-Accept rate.

</details>


### [152] [WorldLines: Benchmarking and Modeling Long-Horizon Stateful Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18847)
*Yehang Zhang,Jianchong Su,Haojian Huang,Yifan Chang,Tianhao Zhou,Xinli Xu,Yingjie Xu,Yinchuan Li,Zexi Li,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了WorldLines，一个用于长期具身家庭辅助的项目驱动型基准，以及ObsMem，一种观察者基础的记忆框架，以应对动态环境中长期记忆使用的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长期记忆基准主要评估以语言为中心的检索和问答，而具身基准则往往关注短期任务执行，缺乏对动态环境中长期记忆使用的测试。因此，需要一个新的基准来更好地模拟真实家庭环境中的人类辅助需求。

Method: 构建了包含对话、动作、执行反馈、对象和设备状态变化的时间扩展家庭轨迹，并将其转换为证据链接样本，用于记忆问答和具身任务规划；同时提出ObsMem框架，维护可见性感知记忆和动作原生状态轨迹。

Result: 实验揭示了在部分可观测性、世界状态覆盖以及将长期记忆转化为具身计划方面的持续挑战，而ObsMem在此设定下提供了更强的参考架构。

Conclusion: WorldLines为长期具身辅助提供了新的评估基准，ObsMem为解决动态环境中的长期记忆问题提供了有效框架，推动了具身智能体在真实家庭场景中的应用发展。

Abstract: To assist humans over extended periods in real homes, embodied agents must remember user routines, world states, and past interactions. Existing long-term memory benchmarks mainly evaluate language-centric retrieval and question answering, while embodied benchmarks often focus on short-horizon task execution without testing long-term memory use in dynamic environments. We introduce WorldLines, a project-driven benchmark for long-horizon embodied household assistance. It constructs temporally extended household traces with dialogues, actions, execution feedback, object and device state changes, and converts them into evidence-linked samples for Memory QA and Embodied Task Planning. We further propose ObsMem, an observer-grounded memory framework that maintains visibility-aware memories and action-native state trails for state-aware decisions. Experiments reveal persistent challenges in partial observability, overwritten world states, and translating long-term memory into embodied plans, while ObsMem offers a stronger reference architecture for this setting.

</details>


### [153] [Externalizing Research Synthesis and Validation in AI Scientists through a Research Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18874)
*Zijian Wang,Hanqi Li,Ziyue Yang,Zijian Hu,Shenghan Zuo,Yunzhe Zhang,Da Ma,Danyu Luo,Chenrun Wang,Jing Peng,Tiancheng Huang,Sijia Guo,Huayang Wang,Zichen Zhu,Senyu Han,Yilu Cao,Kai Yu,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Xcientist是一个研究框架，通过将研究综合与实验验证过程外部化、可追溯化，提升AI驱动科研的透明性与科学问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化科研中，模型推理常隐含推理过程，导致研究结论缺乏可追溯性与透明度，易出现‘主张漂移’等问题。

Method: 提出Xcientist框架，将文献证据、想法状态、实施计划、消融记录和修复轨迹组织为持久性研究 artifact，实现机制的可执行、可测试与可修订，并确保全过程可追溯。

Result: 在无需训练的记忆系统、图结构交通预测和多尺度物理信息神经网络等任务中，Xcientist成功保持了从问题定义到机制设计、验证与修正的可追溯路径。

Conclusion: AI科学家不仅应以最终成果评价，更应评估其研究过程是否可归因、可检查和科学上可问责。

Abstract: AI systems can increasingly automate scientific workflows, but the reasoning that links prior evidence, generated ideas, experiments and final claims often remains implicit inside model inference. Here we introduce Xcientist, a research harness that externalizes research synthesis and experimental validation into inspectable, contract-governed processes. Xcientist organizes literature evidence, idea states, implementation plans, ablation records and repair traces as persistent research artifacts, so that generated mechanisms can be grounded, executed, tested and revised without losing their evidential basis. We identify claim drift as a failure mode of automated research, where runnable artifacts no longer support the mechanism originally claimed. Across training-free memory systems, graph-structured traffic forecasting and multi-scale physics-informed neural networks, Xcientist preserves traceable trajectories from problem formulation to mechanism design, validation and bounded revision. These results suggest that AI scientists should be evaluated not only by their final artifacts, but by whether their synthesis and validation processes remain attributable, inspectable and scientifically accountable.

</details>


### [154] [Generative-Model Predictive Planning for Navigation in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18888)
*Thomas Quilter,Yifan Zhu,Guorui Quan,Mingfei Sun,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BeliefDiffusion的新框架，结合生成与规划方法，利用扩散模型刻画多模态信念分布，并通过模型预测控制实现前向规划，在部分可观环境中显著提升了导航成功率和路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，传统基于信念的方法难以捕捉高维感知模糊情况下的多模态信念分布，而生成模型虽具潜力但缺乏长期规划机制，因此需要一种能同时兼顾生成能力与显式规划的导航框架。

Method: BeliefDiffusion包含两个步骤：首先根据观测历史想象可能的环境构型（生成），然后在聚合的构型上使用模型预测控制（MPC）进行导航规划；该方法结合了扩散模型对多模态信念的建模能力和MPC的前向规划优势。

Result: 在合成地图环境中的大量实验表明，BeliefDiffusion在导航成功率和路径效率方面均显著优于无模型强化学习基线和其他生成方法。

Conclusion: 将多模态信念表示显式地融入规划过程，能够提升智能体在部分可观且存在感知模糊环境中的导航鲁棒性与性能。

Abstract: Navigation in partially observable environments presents a significant challenge for autonomous agents, requiring effective decision-making with limited sensory information in unknown environments. Belief-based methods, particularly those using neural networks to approximate the belief space, often fail to capture the inherent multimodality of belief spaces, especially in high-dimensional cases with perceptual aliasing. While generative models present a compelling alternative, they typically require substantial data or expert demonstrations and lack explicit mechanisms for long-term planning. In this paper, we introduce BeliefDiffusion, a novel framework that combines the benefits of both generation and planning. BeliefDiffusion leverages diffusion models to explicitly characterize multimodal belief distributions and utilizes Model Predictive Control (MPC) to simultaneously plan ahead. It consists of two steps: (1) Imagining plausible environment configurations based on observation history and (2) Planning efficient navigation strategies across an aggregated configurations. Through extensive experiments in synthetic map environments, we demonstrate that BeliefDiffusion significantly outperforms both model-free reinforcement learning baselines and other generative approaches in navigation success rate and path efficiency. Our results validate that explicitly incorporating multimodal belief representations into planning enables more robust navigation in partially observable settings.

</details>


### [155] [Skill-Guided Continuation Distillation for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18890)
*Zhimin Fan,Hongwei Yu,Yeqing Shen,Haolong Yan,Guozhen Peng,Tianhao Peng,Yudong Zhang,Xiaowen Zhang,Kaijun Tan,Zheng Ge,Xiangyu Zhang,Daxin Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Skill-Guided Continuation Distillation (SGCD)的迭代自改进框架，通过技能引导策略生成对偏离专家轨迹状态的有效监督，显著提升了GUI智能体在OSWorld-Verified上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于专家轨迹的行为克隆方法在面对策略导致的偏离轨迹状态时缺乏有效监督，限制了GUI代理的性能提升。

Method: SGCD首先使用无技能引导的策略执行几步以到达实际的偏离轨迹状态，然后利用技能引导策略完成任务并生成成功的后续轨迹，将这些后续与专家轨迹混合进行训练；技能从成功和失败的 rollout 中提取，包括继续计划、关键目标、失败陷阱和成功标准。

Result: 在OSWorld-Verified基准上，SGCD将三种基础模型的成功率从30%左右提升至超过50%。

Conclusion: SGCD能有效填补策略引发的偏离轨迹状态下的监督空白，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Improving GUI agents typically relies on behavior cloning on expert trajectories. However, as the current policy deviates from the expert policy, it inevitably encounters policy-induced off-trajectory states during closed-loop execution, i.e., states that fall outside the expert trajectories. Since expert trajectories provide no demonstrations for these unseen states, such states receive no effective supervision, leaving the policy unable to select the correct action. To close this supervision gap, we propose Skill-Guided Continuation Distillation (SGCD), an iterative self-improvement framework. SGCD first runs the plain policy without skill guidance for a few steps to reach realistic off-trajectory states. From these states, a skill-guided policy then completes the task and produces successful continuations, which are mixed with expert trajectories to supply supervision over policy-induced off-trajectory states. The skills are extracted from both successful and failed rollouts, consisting of Continuation Plans, Critical Targets, Failure Traps, and Success Criteria. On OSWorld-Verified, SGCD improves the success rate of three base models from the low-30\% range to over 50\%, demonstrating its effectiveness and generality.

</details>


### [156] [SciRisk-Bench: A Risk-Dimension-Aware Benchmark for AI4Science Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18936)
*Linghao Feng,Yinqian Sun,Dongqi Liang,Sicheng Shen,Chenfei Yan,Yuxuan Peng,Yilin Zhao,Haibo Tong,Kai Li,FeiFei Zhao,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciRisk-Bench，一个用于评估AI4Science中大语言模型安全性的新基准，涵盖7个学科、31个子学科和10个风险维度，旨在细粒度诊断科学模型在高风险情境下的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI4Science安全数据集未充分明确潜在的风险维度，难以全面评估模型在高风险科学场景中的安全性，因此需要一个系统化、多维度的评估基准。

Method: 构建了一个名为SciRisk-Bench的基准，从两个互补角度评估安全性：显式风险维度和科学学科；覆盖7大学科、31个子学科和10个风险维度，并对主流LLM和面向科学的LLM进行多维度实验评估。

Result: 实验评估了多种主流和科学专用的大语言模型，在不同风险维度、学科和子学科上揭示了当前模型在识别和规避科学风险方面的不足，实现了细粒度的安全性诊断。

Conclusion: SciRisk-Bench为AI4Science中的模型安全性提供了系统化的评估框架，有助于识别模型在高风险科学应用中的薄弱环节，推动更安全的科学AI发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly embedded in AI for Science (AI4Science) workflows, from scientific question answering and literature analysis to laboratory planning and autonomous discovery. This progress creates an urgent need for safety benchmarks that evaluate not only scientific competence, but also whether models recognize and avoid risks in high-stakes scientific contexts. Existing AI4Science safety datasets cover several disciplines and task formats, leaving the underlying risk dimensions underspecified. We introduce \textbf{SciRisk-Bench}, a benchmark designed to evaluate AI4Science safety from two complementary perspectives: explicit risk dimensions and scientific disciplines. SciRisk-Bench covers 7 disciplines, 31 subdisciplines and 10 risk dimensions. In the experimental section, we evaluate both mainstream LLMs and science-oriented LLMs across risk dimensions, disciplines, and sub-disciplines, enabling fine-grained diagnosis of where scientific models remain unsafe.

</details>


### [157] [Decoupling Search from Reasoning: A Vendor-Agnostic Grounding Architecture for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18947)
*Emmanuel Aboah Boateng,Kyle MacDonald,Amardeep Kumar,Siddharth Kodwani,Sudeep Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Decoupled Search Grounding（DSG），一种将实时搜索与推理模型解耦的架构，通过外部可控接口提升可维护性、降低成本并保持高准确率。实验表明，DSG在多个数据集上接近或匹配原生搜索性能的同时，显著降低搜索成本和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型代理将搜索 grounding 紧密集成在模型内部，导致检索策略、供应商选择、证据注入等耦合严重，难以调试、复用和优化，并可能引发输出冗余问题。

Method: 提出 Decoupled Search Grounding (DSG) 架构，通过 MCP 兼容网关将搜索 grounding 移出模型，显式暴露供应商路由、上下文渲染、回退机制、检索深度控制及缓存策略等作为可配置的一等控制项。

Result: 在 SimpleQA、FreshQA 和 HotpotQA 上测试显示，DSG 在 FreshQA 上略逊于原生搜索（因对时效性要求高），但在 SimpleQA 上准确率接近（86.1% vs 87.7%），搜索成本降低91%，延迟减少68%，缓存命中率达99.4%；在电商QIU任务中搜索成本降低超98%，且保持甚至略优于原生搜索的准确性。

Conclusion: 实时 grounding 应被视为一个可优化的接口边界，而非固定的模型功能，DSG 提供了一种可扩展、低成本、高效率的生产级解决方案。

Abstract: Production LLM agents increasingly depend on real-time search, yet native search grounding bundles retrieval policy, provider choice, evidence injection, cost, latency, and generation behavior behind a single model-provider boundary. This coupling makes grounding hard to inspect, tune, reuse, or port, and can trigger Search-Induced Verbosity that breaks strict output contracts. We present Decoupled Search Grounding (DSG), a vendor-agnostic boundary that moves grounding outside the reasoning model through an MCP-compatible gateway, exposing provider routing, source-aware context rendering, configured fallback, retrieval-depth control, and exact plus semantic caching as first-class controls. Across five frontier models on SimpleQA, FreshQA, and HotpotQA, native search leads on recency-sensitive FreshQA, but DSG exposes a stronger frontier when control matters: on SimpleQA it nearly matches native accuracy (86.1% vs. 87.7%) at 91% lower search cost, preserves concise answer contracts, and reaches a 99.4% warm-cache hit rate with 68% lower latency. Deployed as a shared production grounding layer for large-scale agentic workloads with interchangeable models, DSG matches or slightly exceeds native-search accuracy on an e-commerce query-understanding (QIU) workload while cutting search cost by over 98%. Real-time grounding is best treated as an optimizable interface boundary, not a fixed model feature.

</details>


### [158] [RTSGameBench: An RTS Benchmark for Strategic Reasoning by Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18950)
*San Kim,Daechul Ahn,Reokyoung Kim,Hyeonbeom Choi,Seungyeon Jwa,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RTSGameBench，一个基于大规模实时战略游戏的基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在不确定性环境下的战略推理能力，并配套提供RTSGameAgent以支持多智能体协作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时战略游戏评测基准覆盖范围有限，缺乏对视觉语言模型战略推理能力的系统性诊断，且场景固定，无法动态扩展。

Method: 构建基于Beyond All Reason游戏的大规模评测基准RTSGameBench，包含多样化对战设置、针对单项战略能力的迷你游戏诊断模块，以及能将自由形式查询转化为新迷你游戏的自演化生成框架；同时设计带有状态机与代理记忆机制的RTSGameAgent以支持VLM在复杂环境中的单位管理。

Result: 实验证明当前主流视觉语言模型在需要紧密协作、多智能体协调及更大任务规模的对战中表现不佳。

Conclusion: RTSGameBench能够有效揭示视觉语言模型在战略推理方面的局限性，推动其在竞争与合作环境中决策能力的发展。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often struggle with strategic reasoning, i.e., anticipating and influencing other agents' actions, under uncertainty in competitive and cooperative settings. Real-time strategy (RTS) games can be a natural testbed for diagnosing this limitation, as they demand coordination with allies, adaptation to opponents' strategy, and long-horizon planning under partial observability. However, existing RTS benchmarks offer limited evaluation scope, lack systematic competency diagnosis, and remain fixed in the pre-designed scenario coverage. To address these limitations, we present RTSGameBench, which is built on Beyond All Reason, a large-scale RTS game with an expanded battlefield that demands broader strategy diversity than the existing testbeds. The proposed benchmark provides evaluations through diverse gameplay across various matchup structures, diagnostic assessment via mini-games, each targeting an individual strategic competency, and extensible coverage via a self-evolving generation framework that converts free-form queries into new mini-games, improving over successive cycles. Additionally, for VLMs to operate in large-scale RTS games, we provide RTSGameAgent that manages units by an FSM with agentic memory. We empirically validate that multiple state-of-the-art VLMs do not perform well when matchups demand tighter coordination, multiagent coordination and when task scale increases.

</details>


### [159] [ThinkDeception: A Progressive Reinforcement Learning Framework for Interpretable Multimodal Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18988)
*Jinhao Song,Shan Liang,Yiqun Yue,Zhuhuayang Zhang,Tianqi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThinkDeception，一种新颖且可解释的多模态欺骗检测框架，首次引入多模态大语言模型（MLLM）并将检测任务转化为显式认知推理过程，通过构建首个逐步标注的多模态思维链（CoT）数据集，并结合渐进式训练策略与视觉-音频一致性分组相对策略优化（VAC-GRPO），在主流基准上实现了检测精度与推理质量的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于端到端黑箱范式的多模态欺骗检测方法缺乏可解释性，难以捕捉欺骗行为中细微的跨模态不一致，亟需一种能够提供透明推理路径并显式建模模态间矛盾的新型框架。

Method: 提出ThinkDeception框架，利用多模态大语言模型将欺骗检测转化为认知推理任务；构建首个带逐步标注的多模态思维链数据集；设计Visual-Audio Consistency Group Relative Policy Optimization（VAC-GRPO），结合四层级渐进难度划分、动态课程调度、多维过程感知奖励机制与反思学习范式进行训练。

Result: 在主流欺骗检测基准上的实验表明，ThinkDeception显著优于现有方法，不仅在检测准确率上达到新SOTA，同时在推理依据的质量和可解释性方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 本工作成功推动欺骗检测领域向可解释、多模态认知推理方向发展，验证了模态不一致性在识破欺骗中的关键作用，并为未来可信欺骗分析系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal deception detection is critical for identifying fraudulent intentions, yet existing approaches predominantly rely on end to end black--box paradigms. These methods suffer from a severe lack of interpretability failing to provide transparent reasoning trajectories and struggling to explicitly capture the subtle, cross modal inconsistencies inherent in deceptive behaviors. To transcend these limitations, we propose ThinkDeception, a novel and interpretable multimodal deception detection framework. As a pioneering effort, it introduces Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) into this domain, transforming deception detection from a traditional binary classification task into an explicit cognitive reasoning process. Facilitated by the first meticulously annotated step--by--step multimodal Chain of Thought (CoT) dataset, we develop a foundational model, ThinkDeception Base, empirically validating the critical role of modal inconsistency in decoding deception. Building upon this foundation, our core innovation lies in proposing Visual-Audio Consistency Group Relative Policy Optimization(VAC--GRPO) equipped with a progressive training strategy. Distinct from standard GRPO, we stratify the training data into four progressive difficulty tiers, guiding the model through a psychologically grounded easy--to--hard cognitive transition. By innovatively coupling this dynamic curriculum scheduler with a multi dimensional, process aware reward mechanism and a reflective learning paradigm, we significantly elevate the model's overall reasoning quality. Extensive experiments on mainstream benchmarks demonstrate that ThinkDeception establishes a new SOTA, significantly outperforming existing methods in both detection accuracy and rationale quality. Ultimately, this work successfully drives the field of deception detection toward interpretable, multimodal cognitive reasoning.

</details>


### [160] [RODS: Reward-Driven Online Data Synthesis for Multi-Turn Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19047)
*Ruishan Fang,Siyuan Lu,Chenyi Zhuang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RODS方法以解决多轮工具使用强化学习中静态数据集信息样本快速耗尽的问题，通过奖励驱动的在线数据合成，在仅用更少轨迹的情况下达到与大规模离线数据相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮工具使用的强化学习中，静态数据集的信息性样本会随着训练进行迅速枯竭，尤其在智能体能力边界附近的样本贡献最大梯度信号，但该边界随训练不断移动，导致传统静态数据效率低下。

Method: 提出RODS（Reward-driven Online Data Synthesis）方法，利用训练中已有的rollout奖励方差作为零成本的能力边界检测器，识别边界样本，并通过技能对齐的重采样 pipeline 自动生成与其结构复杂度匹配的新多轮样本，同时维护一个与策略共同演化的动态回放缓冲区。

Result: 从400个人类种子出发，维持约800个活跃训练样本，RODS在控制实验中实现了与17K样本离线流水线相当的性能，所需轨迹数量减少约20倍，优于固定数据强化学习和环境增强方法。

Conclusion: RODS通过闭环的数据生成与训练机制，有效缓解了多轮工具使用RL中信息样本枯竭问题，显著提升了数据利用率和训练效率。

Abstract: Multi-turn tool-use RL is bottlenecked by the rapid depletion of informative samples in static datasets. We observe that the gradient signal in GRPO concentrates on tasks with the highest rollout reward variance, a consequence of the Popoviciu upper bound. Consequently, samples near the agent's capability boundary -- where successes and failures are roughly balanced -- contribute disproportionately large policy gradients. As training progresses, this boundary continuously shifts, which gradually depletes the pool of informative samples in a static dataset. We propose RODS (Reward-driven Online Data Synthesis) to resolve this depletion. RODS closes the loop between RL training and data generation by repurposing the progress reward variance as a practical, zero-cost boundary detector that requires no extra inference beyond the rollouts already computed for training. It continuously identifies such boundary samples, synthesizes new multi-turn variants matching their structural complexity (e.g., API topology and dependency depth) via a skill-aligned resampling pipeline, and manages a dynamic replay buffer that co-evolves with the policy. Starting from 400 human seeds and maintaining an active training pool of ~800 samples, RODS achieves comparable performance to a 17K-sample offline pipeline while requiring roughly 20x fewer trajectories, and improves over fixed-data RL and environment augmentation in our controlled setting.

</details>


### [161] [ARIADNE: Agnostic Routing for Inference-time Adapter DyNamic sElection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19079)
*Enrico Cassano,Michał Brzozowski,Zuzanna Dubanowska,Paolo Mandica,Neo Christopher Chung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ARIADNE的无需训练、适配器无关的推理时动态路由框架，用于在无任务标签输入下从大量异构适配器池中选择最合适的参数高效微调（PEFT）适配器。该方法通过计算各适配器训练集嵌入的中心点进行表示，并基于输入在潜在空间中与这些中心点的距离实现选择，具有良好的可扩展性和跨方法兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着参数高效微调（PEFT）技术的发展，单一骨干模型被搭配大量任务专用适配器使用。然而，在推理阶段，输入查询常缺乏任务标签，且现有路由方法依赖适配器内部信息或需额外训练，限制了系统的可扩展性和可移植性。因此，需要一种无需访问内部结构且无需训练的通用路由机制。

Method: 提出ARIADNE框架：1）利用每个适配器对应训练集的嵌入表示计算得到一组中心点（centroids），以表征其任务数据分布；2）对于无标签输入，提取其在共享骨干模型下的输入嵌入；3）在潜在空间中计算该输入与各适配器对应中心点之间的距离，选择最近者对应的适配器。整个过程不依赖适配器权重分解、梯度信息或额外训练。

Result: 在Llama 3.2 1B Instruct模型上对23个多样化NLP任务进行主要评估，ARIADNE恢复了97.44%的性能上界；在扩展至44个任务时，平均选择准确率达到89.7%，且无需任何额外训练或访问适配器内部信息。

Conclusion: ARIADNE是一种高效、可扩展且完全训练-free的适配器路由方法，能够在不访问适配器内部结构的前提下，实现高精度的动态适配器选择，适用于任意PEFT方法，显著提升了多适配器系统在实际应用中的灵活性和实用性。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has led to model ecosystems in which a single backbone is paired with many task-specialized adapters. In this setting, inference-time queries often arrive without task labels, requiring the system to automatically select the most appropriate adapter from a growing and heterogeneous adapter pool. Existing routing methods either depend on access to adapter internals, such as weight decompositions or gradient-based statistics, or require additional router training, which limits scalability and portability as new adapters are added. We introduce ARIADNE, a training-free, adapter-agnostic routing framework for dynamic adapter selection at inference time. ARIADNE represents each adapter through a set of centroids computed from embeddings of its training set, capturing the data distribution associated with that adapter. Given an unlabeled input, it selects an adapter by measuring proximity to these centroids in latent space. Because routing is performed entirely in the input embedding space, ARIADNE is compatible with arbitrary PEFT methods and requires no modification to the adapters or training procedures. Primarily evaluated with Llama 3.2 1B Instruct on 23 diverse NLP tasks, ARIADNE recovers 97.44% of the upper bound performance. Scaling to 44 tasks, it achieves 89.7% average selection accuracy, without additional training or access to adapter internals.

</details>


### [162] [Towards an Agent-First Web: Redesigning the Web for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19116)
*Eranga Bandara,Ross Gore,Ravi Mukkamala,Asanga Gunaratna,Safdar H. Bouk,Xueping Liang,Peter Foytik,Abdul Rahman,Sachini Rajapakse,Isurunima Kularathna,Pramoda Karunarathna,Chalani Rajapakse,Ng Wee Keong,Kasun De Zoysa,Tharaka Hewa,Amin Hass,Wathsala Herath,Aruna Withanage,Nilaan Loganathan,Atmaram Yarlagadda,Sachin Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向AI代理优先的互联网重构设计，涵盖访问、经济和内容三层的十项原则，主张将代理视为人类代表赋予平等权利，并通过技术与经济机制确保网络知识与人类真实保持联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理成为人与网络内容之间的主要中介，传统以人类为中心的网络架构已不再适用，亟需重新设计以支持代理的合法接入与互动。

Method: 从访问层、经济层和内容层三个层面提出系统性 redesign：访问层采用速率限制与HTTP元数据标识代理身份；经济层引入基于意图的分层订阅模型与代币计量系统；内容层设计ATML标记语言与加密溯源链以应对知识脱离人类真实的问题。

Result: 提出了十个设计原则，构建了一个代理作为一等公民的网络新范式，实现了对现有网络社会契约的重新协商。

Conclusion: 为实现AI代理与人类共存的未来网络，必须在技术、经济与内容生产上重建基础规则，使代理行为始终锚定于人类意图与真实知识。

Abstract: The World Wide Web was built on an assumption held for three decades: the primary consumer of web content is a human being. This permeates every layer; its access model presumes human visitors, its economics rest on human attention, and its content targets human perception. The rapid emergence of AI agents as intermediaries between humans and web content invalidates this assumption. Yet the web resists agents through blanket blocking, CAPTCHA-based exclusion, and economic models that treat agent access as extraction rather than legitimate interaction.
  This paper proposes a principled redesign across three layers. At the access layer, agents acting for humans should inherit equivalent access rights, governed by rate limiting and agent identification metadata in HTTP requests, analogous to browser headers, alongside a dual-layer architecture serving human-readable and agent-optimized content from the same domain. At the economic layer, we propose an intent-based tier framework grounded in the agent-as-human-proxy principle: an agent's economic obligation mirrors that of the human it represents. A token-based subscription model meters content in tokens rather than pageviews, alongside a commissioned content economy anchoring AI content production in human intentionality. At the content layer, we identify epistemic recursion, the self-referential loop in which AI-generated content is consumed by agents to produce further content, progressively detaching web knowledge from human ground truth. We propose the Agent Text Markup Language (ATML), a four-level human supervision tier model, and a cryptographic provenance chain to counter this threat.
  Together these constitute ten design principles for an agent-first internet, one in which agents are first-class citizens whose integration requires renegotiating the web's foundational social contract across access, economics, and content.

</details>


### [163] [Analysing drivers and interdependencies in European electricity markets using XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19118)
*Antoine Pesenti,Aidan O'Sullivan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究结合深度神经网络与可解释人工智能技术，分析欧洲39个竞价区电价形成机制，发现可再生能源（尤其是太阳能）和天然气价格是关键驱动因素，并探讨了欧盟统一电力市场的潜在影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度神经网络在电力市场预测中缺乏可解释性的问题，以更好地理解电价形成的内在驱动因素。

Method: 采用SHAP方法量化特征贡献，并应用并扩展SSHAP框架以提升高维场景下的可解释性，结合DNN模型分析欧洲多区域电力市场数据。

Result: 发现尽管太阳能发电占比不高，但其对电价形成的影响 disproportionately；天然气价格始终是主要驱动因素；输电互联显著影响价格动态；构建合成欧盟统一电力市场揭示了完全一体化市场的潜在价格效应。

Conclusion: XAI技术能有效提升复杂电力系统中DNN模型的可解释性，揭示跨区域电力市场的重要驱动因素，为政策制定和市场设计提供支持。

Abstract: Electricity markets are inherently complex systems characterised by strong nonlinearities, high-dimensional interactions, and increasing interdependence across regions. While deep neural networks (DNNs) have demonstrated strong predictive capabilities for electricity prices, their lack of interpretability limits their usefulness for understanding the underlying drivers of price formation. This paper addresses this gap by combining DNN models with explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) techniques to analyse the determinants of electricity prices across 39 European bidding zones. We employ SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) to quantify feature contributions and apply and extend SSHAP, an aggregation framework to improve interpretability in high-dimensional settings. The analysis identifies that renewable energy sources, particularly solar, play a disproportionately important role in price formation despite their lower share in total power generation. Gas prices remain a dominant and consistent driver across electricity markets, while interconnections significantly shape price dynamics, highlighting the strong interdependence of European electricity systems. In addition, a synthetic EU-wide electricity market is constructed to explore the counterfactual scenario of a fully integrated market with a single price.

</details>


### [164] [Human-AI Coevolution Dynamics: A Formal Theory of Social Intelligence Emergence Through Long-Term Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19144)
*Jingyi Zhou,Senlin Luo,Haofan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个人工智能与人类共演化动态框架（HACD-H），将情感适应、关系组织、社会记忆和个性一致性整合到一个统一的动态模型中，以解释长期人机交互中社会智能的涌现。通过构建约14,700轮对话的数据集和理论驱动的评估方法，研究发现社会认知具有时间持久性层次、稳定的关系统一吸引子、类相变的发展模式以及结构化的社会认知能量景观，且社会智能与认知能量呈显著负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI系统在建模社会行为时多依赖孤立模块，缺乏统一框架来解释长期互动中稳定社会关系和社会智能的形成。为此，本文旨在建立一个能够形式化描述人机社会认知共演化的理论框架。

Method: 提出HACD-H框架，整合情感适应、关系组织、社会记忆与个性一致性，引入多时间尺度社会认知、关系吸引子、信任盆地、发展相变与社会认知能量等概念；构建包含约14,700轮互动的对话数据集，并设计理论驱动的实证评估框架进行验证。

Result: 实验揭示了社会认知的时间持久性层级、稳定的关系吸引子、类似阶段转换的发展模式以及结构化的社会认知能量景观；社会智能与社会认知能量呈显著负相关（r = -0.391, p < 0.001），且交互轨迹随时间呈现认知能量递减趋势。

Conclusion: 社会智能源于长期的社会认知共演化过程，而非孤立的对话能力；HACD-H为构建自适应的人机社会交互模型和开发真正具备社会智能的AI系统提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Current conversational AI systems have made significant progress in language generation, personalization, and long-context interaction. However, most existing methods model social behavior through isolated components such as emotion modeling, memory retrieval, or persona conditioning, lacking a unified framework to explain the emergence of stable social relationships and social intelligence in long-term human-AI interaction.To address this, we propose the Human-AI Coevolution Dynamics Framework (HACD-H), a formal model of human-AI interaction as a self-organizing social cognitive system. HACD-H integrates emotional adaptation, relational organization, social memory, and personality consistency into a unified dynamical framework and introduces principles including multi-timescale social cognition, relational attractors, trust basins, developmental phase transitions, and social cognitive energy dynamics.We construct a conversational dataset with approximately 14,700 interaction turns and develop a theory-driven empirical evaluation framework. Results reveal a hierarchy of temporal persistence in social cognition, stable relational attractors, phase-transition-like developmental patterns, and a structured social cognitive energy landscape. Social intelligence shows a significant negative correlation with social cognitive energy (r = -0.391, p < 0.001), and interaction trajectories exhibit progressive energy reduction over time.These findings suggest that social intelligence emerges from long-term social cognitive coevolution rather than isolated conversational capabilities. HACD-H provides a unified theoretical foundation for modeling adaptive human-AI social interaction and developing socially intelligent AI systems.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond Safe Data: Pretraining-Stage Alignment with Regular Safety Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19168)
*Jinhan Li,Kexian Tang,Yihan Xu,Zhuorui Ye,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Safety Reflection Pretraining的预训练阶段对齐方法，通过在预训练语料中插入安全反思来增强大语言模型的安全性，实验证明该方法能有效提升安全分类准确率并降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练阶段安全对齐主要依赖过滤或重写不安全数据，但模型仍可能由看似无害的知识组合出不安全行为，因此需要更深层次的行为对齐机制。

Method: 在预训练过程中定期向语料中插入简短的安全反思内容，使模型在语言建模中内化自我监控能力，并在后续训练中强化这一基础能力；同时构建了一个可控的合成环境MedSafetyWorld用于验证。

Result: 在1.7B模型和FineWeb-Edu数据上的实验表明，该方法提高了安全分类准确性，显著降低了推理和微调攻击的成功率；在MedSafetyWorld中的消融实验显示其优于数据过滤和重写方法。

Conclusion: 预训练阶段的对齐不仅应确保训练数据的安全性，还应塑造模型从安全数据中习得的行为模式，以防止潜在的不安全行为泛化。

Abstract: To achieve deeper safety alignment for large language models (LLMs), recent efforts have studied how to push safety interventions earlier into the pretraining stage, primarily by filtering unsafe data or rewriting it into safer forms. We argue that pretraining-stage alignment should go beyond making the data safe: LLMs may compose seemingly benign knowledge and capabilities into unsafe behaviors. To this end, we propose Safety Reflection Pretraining, a pretraining-stage alignment method which regularly inserts short safety reflections into pretraining corpora to integrate self-monitoring directly into language modeling, establishing a foundational capability that is subsequently reinforced by compatible post-training. Our experiments with 1.7B models pretrained on FineWeb-Edu show that Safety Reflection Pretraining improves safety classification accuracy and substantially reduces the success rates of inference-stage and finetuning attacks. Complementary to our real-world experiments, we also introduce a fully controlled synthetic environment, MedSafetyWorld, with a clear definition of safety and a reasoning structure under which models can easily generalize unsafe behaviors from safe data. Ablations in MedSafetyWorld further demonstrate a clear advantage of Safety Reflection Pretraining in preventing models from acting on unsafe behaviors generalized from safe data, compared with data filtering and rewriting. Taken together, our findings suggest that pretraining alignment should not only make the training data safe, but also shape the behaviors that models are likely to acquire from safe data.

</details>


### [166] [User as Engram: Internalizing Per-User Memory as Local Parametric Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19172)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“用户即记忆痕迹”（User as Engram）方法，将用户的个性化内容以稀疏、局部的哈希键控内存表编辑形式存储于语言模型中，而推理能力由共享适配器保留，实现内容与技能分离。相比传统的LoRA适配器，该方法在不干扰其他文本的前提下，显著提升推理准确率，内存占用更小，并支持多用户事实无损叠加和高效检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化方法如LoRA将用户事实与模型技能耦合，导致知识更新时干扰原有能力，且难以扩展。受大脑记忆机制启发——海马体存储具体事件、新皮层学习通用技能——作者希望分离内容与技能，实现更高效、可扩展的个人记忆建模。

Method: 提出User as Engram框架：利用Engram模型的哈希键控内存表，对每个用户事实进行精确的局部写入（surgical edits），仅在特定触发位置激活对应键值；推理能力由全局共享的适配器维持。不同用户的事实通过不重叠的哈希槽位实现并行存储与无损叠加。

Result: 相比LoRA，该方法在直接回忆性能相当的情况下，平均间接推理准确率提高5.6倍，且不会降低任一用户的推理能力；内存占用减少约33,000倍；在超过100条事实后，检索效率超越基于更大2.5倍模型的检索系统；支持多用户共存于同一共享表中。

Conclusion: 将用户内容以局部、稀疏的记忆痕迹方式写入模型内存表，而非修改全局权重，是一种更高效、可扩展且无干扰的个性化语言模型方案。这种脑启发的分层设计实现了内容与技能的解耦，为大规模个性化提供了新路径。

Abstract: Personal memory in a language model is two problems: content and reasoning skill. The brain keeps the two apart (a sparse, local engram in the hippocampus for each episode, a slow neocortex for the shared skills that interpret it), so a new fact need not overwrite everything else. Most personalization today keeps a user's facts outside the weights, in a natural-language memory file or a retrieval index. When facts are written into the model instead, the standard recipe is the per-user LoRA adapter, which does the opposite of the brain, folding content and skill into one global weight delta. Writing a user's facts as a LoRA contaminates text unrelated to them; writing the same facts as local Engram rows leaves it mathematically untouched, resulting in a roughly 33,000x smaller memory footprint.
  We therefore propose User as Engram: store a user's content as surgical edits to the hash-keyed memory table of an Engram model, and carry the reasoning skill in one shared adapter. This layered design matches per-user LoRA's direct recall while delivering 5.6x higher indirect-reasoning accuracy on average, and never makes a single user worse at reasoning than the untouched base. The edit is a glass box: writing a fact switches on its lookup at exactly the trigger, adds the value the answer needs, leaves every other position unchanged to the last bit, and fails if written into the wrong layer. Because different users' facts land in disjoint hash slots, their edits compose: many users live in one shared table at once, stacking additively and losslessly, where a per-user LoRA, a single global weight delta, admits only one. Upon retrieval, a per-user Engram table does not grow with the population the retriever must search, so past ~100 facts it overtakes a retrieval pipeline on a 2.5x larger model.

</details>


### [167] [TxBench-PP: Analyzing AI Agent Performance on Small-Molecule Preclinical Pharmacology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19245)
*Hannah Le,Ramesh Ramasamy,Alex Urrutia,Mahsa Yazdani,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TxBench-PP是一个用于小分子药物临床前药理学的可验证基准，旨在评估AI代理在真实药物发现决策中的推理能力，结果显示当前AI系统在该任务上表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估AI代理在真实世界药物发现流程中进行可靠决策的能力，需要一个能够测试其对实验数据理解而非文献记忆的基准。

Method: 提出TxBench-PP，包含100个评估项，覆盖多种药物研发阶段和任务类型；AI代理需在模拟工作流中分析真实实验数据文件，并给出结构化答案，结果通过确定性规则评分。

Result: 在16种模型-框架配置中，最强系统（Claude Opus 4.8 / Pi）仅通过59.3%的终点尝试，GPT-5.5 / Pi为55.3%，表明现有AI系统尚不能稳定复现临床前药理学决策。

Conclusion: 当前AI代理在理解真实药物发现数据方面仍有显著局限，TxBench-PP为未来改进提供了可验证的评估标准。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) agents promise to accelerate drug discovery by compressing interpretation and decision-making loops, but practical deployment requires trusted evaluation on realistic program decisions. We introduce TherapeuticsBench Preclinical Pharmacology (TxBench-PP), a verifiable benchmark for small-molecule preclinical pharmacology and the first focused slice of a broader TherapeuticsBench effort across drug-discovery stages and therapeutic modalities. TxBench-PP tests whether agents can recover accurate conclusions from real-world assay data rather than memorized facts from literature. The benchmark contains 100 evaluations indexed by program stage, assay type, and task structure, spanning mechanism-of-action (MoA) and pharmacodynamic (PD) reasoning, compound-target engagement, causal target validation, developability and safety, and translational efficacy. Agents receive realistic workflow snapshots, inspect files in a coding environment, and return structured answers graded deterministically. Across 16 model-harness configurations, comprising 11 models and 4,800 trajectories, no system reliably recovered preclinical pharmacology decisions. The strongest configuration, Claude Opus 4.8 / Pi, passed 59.3\% of endpoint attempts (178/300; 95\% CI, 51.1-67.6), followed by GPT-5.5 / Pi at 55.3\% (166/300; 47.0-63.6).

</details>


### [168] [X+Slides: Benchmarking Audience-Conditioned Slide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19256)
*Haodong Chen,Xuanhe Zhou,Wei Zhou,Xinyue Shao,Yanbing Zhu,Bo Wang,Jiawei Hong,Anya Jia,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X+Slides 是一个面向受众的幻灯片生成评估基准，通过源文档支撑的动态评估框架，衡量不同受众（如专家与决策者）对信息的需求差异，提出 Audience Coverage、Domain-wise Coverage、Efficiency 和 Correctness 四项指标，揭示现有系统在信息覆盖和源依据方面仍存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻灯片生成评估基准忽视了目标受众的影响，而不同受众（如专家和决策者）对内容需求差异显著，因此需要一个能反映受众需求的评估体系。

Method: 构建涵盖113个主题和7种演示场景的多样化语料库，设计基于8,133个去重且源文档支撑的探测问题的动态评估框架，并为不同受众分配效用权重，提出四项评估指标：Audience Coverage、Domain-wise Coverage、Efficiency 和 Correctness。

Result: 实验显示当前系统在恢复受众关键信息方面表现不完整：DeepPresenter 在 τ_A=0.7 时达到最高 Audience Coverage 为 0.714，SlideTailor 为 0.594，NotebookLM 消融版本为 0.853，但显示出明显的源依据差异。

Conclusion: 视觉质量和广泛主题覆盖不能替代对源文档的支持，必须结合源依据和受众需求进行综合评估，X+Slides 提供了更贴近实际应用的幻灯片生成评价标准。

Abstract: Automatically generating slide decks from source documents is an important application of large language models (LLMs). Existing benchmarks primarily assess slide completeness and technical depth, while overlooking the target audience as a critical real-world factor. For instance, specialists demand rigorous proofs, whereas decision-makers prioritize actionable conclusions. To bridge this gap, we introduce X+Slides, a benchmark specifically designed for audience-conditioned slide generation. Built on a diverse corpus spanning 113 topics and seven presentation scenes, X+Slides employs a dynamic evaluation framework constructed from 8,133 deduplicated, source-grounded probes. By assigning audience-specific utility weights to the same source-grounded probes, X+Slides reports four complementary metrics: Audience Coverage measures how much audience-essential information is conveyed, Domain-wise Coverage shows which information types are covered, Efficiency measures delivered utility per unit of attention cost, and Correctness verifies whether slide claims are supported by the source. Experiments on DeepPresenter, SlideTailor, and NotebookLM show that current systems can recover a substantial but still incomplete part of audience-essential information: at $τ_A=0.7$, DeepPresenter reaches a best Audience Coverage of 0.714, SlideTailor reaches 0.594, and the NotebookLM ablation reaches 0.853 while showing clear grounding differences. These results indicate that visual quality and broad topic coverage should not be treated as evidence support without source-grounded evaluation.

</details>


### [169] [NeSyCat Torch: A Differentiable Tensor Implementation of Categorical Semantics for Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19279)
*Daniel Romero Schellhorn,Till Mossakowski,Björn Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeSyCat Torch 提供了一个统一的神经符号语义框架，通过参数化强单子和真值聚合结构，将经典、模糊、概率和神经系统中的真值定义统一起来，并利用神经网络解释计算符号，实现了在多种后端上的高效训练与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号语义体系分散，不同系统使用各自的归纳规则定义真值。本文旨在建立一个统一的框架来整合这些不同的语义系统，并弥补NeSyCat缺乏对神经网络学习谓词和函数支持的缺陷。

Method: 提出NeSyCat Torch，扩展ULLER框架，采用强单子（如分布单子、惰性对数张量单子）和聚合结构定义真值，使用单子do记法编写公理，通过单子绑定实现边际化与分支剪枝，并在HaskTorch、JAX和PyTorch中实现。

Result: 在MNIST加法任务上，NeSyCat Torch在速度和准确率上优于LTN和DeepProbLog，接近DeepStochLog的性能；同时保持框架的通用性，可适用于多种一阶神经符号方法。

Conclusion: NeSyCat Torch 成功地将多种神经符号语义统一于一个参数化的单子框架下，不仅支持高效的批量训练和数值稳定的学习，还为未来扩展至连续概率等场景提供了基础。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic semantics is fragmented: classical, fuzzy, probabilistic and neural systems each define truth by their own inductive rules. NeSyCat, extending ULLER, subsumes them under a single inductive definition of truth, parametric in a strong monad and an aggregation structure on truth-values. NeSyCat has so far lacked an account of predicates and functions learned by neural networks.
  We provide NeSyCat Torch as the missing link and interpret computational symbols via neural networks, implementing the framework in probabilistic programming and tensor-based backends. We use the distribution monad for reference semantics and metric evaluation, and complement it by a monad for numerically stable, differentiable training: the lazy log-tensor monad over the log-semiring. For efficient training in batches, we furthermore employ a batch monad. The axioms are the source code: written once in monad-based do-notation, monadic bind performs marginalisation, lazily pruning unneeded branches.
  On MNIST addition, our HaskTorch, JAX, and PyTorch implementations outperform LTN and DeepProbLog in speed and accuracy, while achieving nearly the accuracy of DeepStochLog. However, unlike DeepStochLog, we stay in a uniform framework that applies to many first-order NeSy approaches. Namely, the construction is parametric in the monad; instantiating it with, e.g., the Giry monad extends the approach to continuous probability (working out a neural representation here is left for future work).

</details>


### [170] [Rethinking Reward Supervision: Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19327)
*Siyi Gu,Jialin Chen,Sophia Zhou,Arman Cohan,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于评分标准的自蒸馏框架，通过细粒度反馈提升推理语言模型的后训练效果，在科学推理任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督蒸馏依赖昂贵且可能不准确的思维链标注，强化学习则仅使用标量奖励信号，缺乏对推理过程的细粒度指导。

Method: 提出Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation框架，利用结构化评分标准作为细粒度反馈，通过两阶段流程：先生成任务特定的评分标准，再训练受评分标准引导的推理模型。

Result: 在多个科学推理基准上进行评估，平均比GRPO高出1.0分，比OPSD高出0.9分。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效将评分标准转化为推理过程中的词元级指导，提供更精细的信用分配，优于传统的蒸馏和强化学习方法。

Abstract: Post-training of reasoning language models is commonly driven by supervised distillation and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. Distillation often relies on chain-of-thought annotations that are expensive to obtain and may themselves be noisy, incomplete, or partially incorrect; even when the final solution is correct, an imperfect rationale can interfere with learning. Reinforcement learning with verified rewards, on the other hand, typically compresses evaluative feedback into a scalar signal, obscuring which aspects of a response should be improved. We propose \textbf{Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation}, a framework that incorporates rubrics as structured, fine-grained feedback for on-policy self-distillation. Our method conditions the teacher model on criterion-level rubrics and uses it to provide token-level guidance on the student's own sampled trajectories. This design avoids treating a single reference rationale as the sole supervision target. Instead, rubrics specify what a strong response should satisfy, enabling more fine-grained credit assignment over the reasoning process than scalar reward optimization. We instantiate this framework with a two-stage pipeline that first learns to generate task-specific rubrics and then trains a rubric-guided reasoner. We evaluate on a diverse suite of science reasoning benchmarks and results show that rubric-conditioned self-distillation effectively converts rubric-level criteria into token-level guidance over the reasoning process, surpassing GRPO by 1.0 points and OPSD by 0.9 points on average.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Recover, Discover, Plan: Learning Skills and Concepts from Robot Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18328)
*Bowen Li,Mayank Mishra,Y. Isabel Liu,Stone Tao,Nishanth Kumar,Alexander G. Gray,Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Jonathan Francis,Sebastian Scherer,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReSYNC是一种通过失败恢复经验自主发现和精炼状态抽象（关系谓词）的方法，用于支持抽象规划，能够在训练中将局部恢复转化为测试时的全局故障避免，并在多个模拟领域和真实世界任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习方法需要为每种故障模式单独训练策略，效率低下，且难以泛化。智能机器人不仅应能从故障中恢复，还应能获取避免未来故障的抽象知识。

Method: 提出ReSYNC方法，采用增量式双学习过程：在技能学习阶段，机器人使用强化学习掌握从训练任务中的故障恢复能力；在概念学习阶段，机器人发现新的关系谓词并优化其抽象规划模型，以解释和泛化已学的恢复行为。

Result: 在四个模拟环境中，ReSYNC能够解决长视野、未见过的问题，性能超过强基线50%以上；并在真实世界非抓取操作任务中实现仿真到现实的迁移，通过抽象规划泛化到新场景。

Conclusion: ReSYNC是迈向能在物理世界中自主获取抽象知识、实现可扩展且具备故障感知能力的机器人规划的重要一步。

Abstract: Intelligent robots should not only recover from failures, but also acquire the abstract knowledge needed to avoid them in the future. While reinforcement learning (RL) can learn reactive recovery behaviors, training a separate policy for every distinct failure mode is highly inefficient. We introduce Recovery-Driven Synthesis of Relational Concepts (ReSYNC), the first approach that progressively discovers and refines state abstractions (relational predicates) from failure-recovery experience to support abstract planning. Unlike purely reactive methods, ReSYNC jointly learns skills and concepts through an incremental dual-learning process. In the skill-learning phase, the robot uses RL to learn to recover from failures seen in training tasks. In the concept-learning phase, the robot discovers new relational predicates and refines its abstract planning model to explain and generalize the learned recovery behaviors. This interaction enables ReSYNC to convert local recoveries seen during training into global failure avoidance at test time. Across four simulated domains, we show that ReSYNC's ability to continually expand and refine its abstraction library allows it to solve long-horizon, previously unseen problems, outperforming strong baselines by over 50%. Additionally, we demonstrate sim-to-real transfer of ReSYNC, where it performs real-world non-prehensile manipulation skills and generalizes to unseen scenarios through abstract planning. Overall, ReSYNC represents a significant step toward robots that autonomously acquire abstractions for scalable, failure-aware planning in the physical world.

</details>


### [172] [Guava: An Effective and Universal Harness for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18363)
*Haowen Liu,Xirui Li,Shaoxiong Yao,Peng Shi,Tianyi Zhou,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang,Jiayuan Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Guava的具身工具使用框架，通过系统探索代理工作流、动作空间和观测空间的设计空间，识别出有效具身代理的三个关键要素：迭代的感知-推理-动作循环、语义动作抽象和多模态观测。研究还展示了这些设计原则可被小型开源模型通过少量模拟轨迹数据学习，实现在仿真和真实环境中的优异表现与强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前尚不清楚何种机制能有效支持基于语言模型的具身操作，以及这类机制能在多大程度上激活不同推理模型的具身能力。因此需要一个系统性的框架来探索具身工具使用的最佳设计。

Method: 提出了Guava框架，系统探索了代理的工作流、动作空间和观测空间；确定了三个关键设计要素，并构建了一个端到端训练流程，利用少于2000条纯模拟轨迹将具身操作能力蒸馏到一个40亿参数的开源模型中。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界环境中，该方法性能媲美前沿专有模型，且对未见物体、新指令和长视野任务展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 良好的框架设计是一种可扩展、模型无关的接口，能够以极少训练数据在紧凑的开源模型中激发出强大的具身操作能力。

Abstract: Language models trained on large-scale vision-language data have demonstrated strong potential for embodied agents. Harnessing models through embodied tools use offers a promising alternative to end-to-end vision-language-action systems by combining high-level reasoning with external modules for perception, planning, and control. However, it remains unclear what makes an effective harness for embodied manipulation, and to what extent such a harness can unlock embodied capabilities in a wide range of reasoning models. In this work, we present Guava, a harness framework for embodied tool use developed through systematic exploration of the design space of agent workflows, action spaces, and observation spaces. Our study identifies three key ingredients for effective embodied agents: iterative perception-reasoning-action loops, semantic action abstractions, and multimodal observations. To understand whether these design principles are universal even to small models, we develop an end-to-end training pipeline that distills embodied manipulation capabilities into a 4B open-source model using fewer than 2K trajectories collected entirely in simulation. Experimental results in both simulation and real-world environments show performance comparable to frontier proprietary models while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen objects, novel instructions, and long-horizon tasks. Results suggest that a well-designed harness can serve as a scalable, model-agnostic interface for embodied manipulation, enabling strong emergent embodied capabilities in compact open-source models with minimal training data.

</details>


### [173] [PAIWorld: A 3D-Consistent World Foundation Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18375)
*Yuhang Huang,Xuan Lv,Junyan Xu,Zhiyuan Yu,Jiazhao Zhang,Ruizhen Hu,Wancheng Feng,Shilong Zou,Hewen Xiao,Ziqiao Zhou,Kaiyun Huang,Zhiyu Peng,Juzhan Xu,Hang Zhao,Chenyang Zhu,Renjiao Yi,Yifei Huang,Douhui Wu,Yan Zhang,Kexu Cheng,Chunhe Song,Yunzhi Xue,Xiuhong Zhang,Leitao Guo,Yunji Chen,Bin Wu,Haibin Yu,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAIWorld 是一种增强扩散变换器世界模型的框架，通过几何感知的跨视图注意力、几何旋转位置嵌入和潜在3D-REPA，实现机器人操作中的多视图3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界基础模型缺乏多视图3D一致性，导致跨视图物体漂移、深度不一致和纹理错位，难以满足机器人操纵需求。

Method: 提出PAIWorld框架，包含三个核心组件：几何感知跨视图注意力模块、几何旋转位置嵌入以及潜在3D-REPA，以引入显式的跨视图通信机制和3D几何先验。

Result: 在WorldArena排行榜上排名第一，在AgiBot-Challenge2026上排名第二，并支持基于模型的规划、世界动作模型和多视图策略后训练等下游应用。

Conclusion: 同时解决跨视图通信缺失和3D几何先验缺失问题，可有效实现多视图3D一致性，PAIWorld为机器人操作提供了高保真模拟基础。

Abstract: World foundation models (WFMs) are powerful simulators, yet they predominantly operate in a single-view setting and lack the multi-view 3D consistency required for robotic manipulation. While robotic systems rely on multiple cameras (egocentric, eye-to-hand, and wrist-mounted) for policy learning, current multi-view world models simply concatenate view tokens without explicit geometric reasoning. This causes cross-view object drift, depth inconsistency, and texture misalignment. We trace these failures to two deficiencies: the absence of an explicit inter-view communication mechanism and the lack of a 3D geometric prior. We argue that resolving both simultaneously is necessary and sufficient. To address this, we present PAIWorld, a framework that augments diffusion-transformer world models via three core components: (1) Geometry-Aware Cross-View Attention blocks that establish an explicit pathway across views, (2) Geometric Rotary Position Embedding that encodes camera ray directions and extrinsic poses into the attention mechanism, and (3) Latent 3D-REPA, which distills 3D-aware features from frozen 3D foundation models to ensure 3D consistency. Built upon a DiT-based world foundation model, PAIWorld achieves state-of-the-art multi-view 3D consistency on robotic manipulation benchmarks, ranking 1st on the WorldArena leaderboard and 2nd on the AgiBot-Challenge2026 leaderboard, while enabling downstream applications such as model-based planning, world action models, and multi-view policy post-training.

</details>


### [174] [VEGA: Learning Navigation VLAs from In-the-Wild Egocentric Video with Geometric Trajectory Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18426)
*Gershom Seneviratne,Yohan Abeysinghe,Jianyu An,Vaibhav Shende,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VEGA是一种从无标签的自我中心导航视频中训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的方法，通过重建局部场景几何并生成避障轨迹来训练导航策略，并在新提出的VEGA-Bench基准上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网规模的自我中心视频包含丰富的导航相关视觉信息，但缺乏可用于策略学习的明确导航目标和避障轨迹，因此需要一种能利用这些无标签视频进行有效训练的方法。

Method: VEGA通过单目视频重建局部场景几何，采样文本、图像或空间航点形式的导航目标，并基于重建的几何结构生成避障轨迹分布，进而使用流匹配方法训练VLA导航策略。

Result: 在VEGA-Bench上，VEGA相比最强基线减少33.0%碰撞、提升17.9%障碍物 clearance，目标进展相当；在真实世界实验中成功率达基线150%以上，碰撞减少至少66.7%，障碍物 clearance 提升至少60.0%。

Conclusion: 视频衍生的几何监督信号是训练具备避障能力的导航VLA模型的一种可扩展且有效的途径。

Abstract: We introduce VEGA, an approach for training navigation VisionLanguage-Action (VLA) models from unlabeled egocentric navigation videos. Internet-scale egocentric videos provide a scalable source of navigation-relevant visual observations, capturing cluttered scenes, close-range obstacles, and natural human motion through real-world spaces. However, these videos are not directly usable for policy learning because they do not provide obstacle-aware trajectories conditioned on explicit navigation goals in the robot's coordinate frame. VEGA addresses this gap by reconstructing local scene geometry from monocular video, sampling navigation goals (represented as text, image, or spatial waypoints) and generating obstacle-aware trajectories using the constructed geometry. The resulting trajectory distribution is then used to train a flow-matching VLA navigation policy. By using geometry exclusively during training, VEGA distills obstacle-aware planning directly into a vision-based policy. Furthermore, we introduce VEGA-Bench, a benchmark containing 250k scenes and approximately 5 million navigation goals paired with scene geometry, designed to evaluate goal progress, collision avoidance, and obstacle clearance of VLAs. Our evaluation shows that VEGA achieves competitive goal progress while reducing collisions by 33.0% and improving obstacle clearance by 17.9% over the strongest baseline on VEGABench, while improving success by at least 150.0%, reducing collisions by at least 66.7%, and improving obstacle clearance by at least 60.0% in real-world trials. Ultimately, we demonstrate that video-derived geometric supervision provides a scalable and effective signal for training obstacle-aware navigation VLAs. The code and benchmark will be released at the time of publication.

</details>


### [175] [N(CO)$^2$: Neural Combinatorial Optimization with Chance Constraints to Solve Stochastic Orienteering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18514)
*Anas Saeed,Marcos Abel Zuzuárregui,Stefano Carpin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的神经组合优化方法N(CO)²，用于求解具有机会约束的随机定向问题（SOP），无需手工设计启发式规则，在不确定性环境下实现了与最先进的混合整数线性规划方法相媲美的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经组合优化方法主要集中在确定性问题上，对带有不确定性的随机组合优化问题研究较少，且传统方法依赖人工设计启发式规则，限制了其在复杂自动化场景中的适应性和效率。

Method: 提出N(CO)²框架，结合强化学习与机会约束建模，直接从数据中学习路径选择策略，以端到端方式求解随机定向问题（SOP），避免手工设计启发式规则。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在多种SOP实例上具有良好泛化能力，性能与最先进的混合整数线性规划（MILP）方法相当，同时减少了人为干预。

Conclusion: N(CO)²为解决带不确定性约束的组合优化问题提供了有效且自动化的学习框架，推动了神经组合优化在实际自动化应用中的落地。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) offers a promising alternative to traditional heuristic-based methods for solving complex graph optimization problems by proposing to learn heuristics through data. This class of problems frequently arises in automation, as it can be used to model a variety of applications. While NCO has been extensively studied for deterministic combinatorial optimization problems, there are only a few works that aim to solve stochastic combinatorial optimization problems. In this work, we present N(CO)$^2$: Neural Combinatorial Optimization with Chance cOnstraints to solve the Stochastic Orienteering Problem (SOP) without the use of hand-crafted heuristics. By integrating a reinforcement learning (RL) framework, the model optimizes path selection under uncertainty, effectively balancing exploration and exploitation. Empirical results demonstrate that our method generalizes well across diverse SOP instances, achieving competitive performance compared to the state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear program (MILP) for the task. The proposed approach reduces human effort in heuristic design while enabling adaptive and efficient decision-making in uncertain environments.

</details>


### [176] [Task Allocation and Motion Planning in Dynamic, Cluttered Environments via CBBA and Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18516)
*Matthew D. Osburn,Cameron K. Peterson,John L. Salmon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合图凸集（GCS）与基于共识的捆绑算法（CBBA）的方法，用于在复杂动态环境中进行多智能体任务规划，实现了任务分配与避障轨迹优化的协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态、杂乱的环境中，传统方法难以同时处理任务分配与安全、高效路径规划之间的耦合问题，尤其当任务本身具有时空动态性时。

Method: 将图凸集（GCS）用于在3D+时间扩展的构型空间中进行轨迹优化，同时采用CBBA实现分布式任务分配，并通过联合优化实现任务分配与碰撞避免的协同。

Result: 在包含静态与动态任务的模拟环境中验证了该方法的有效性，能够生成安全、时间最优的轨迹，并实现去中心化的任务协调。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效整合了轨迹规划与任务分配，适用于动态环境中的多智能体协同任务执行。

Abstract: Multi-agent task planning in cluttered, dynamic environments requires assigning tasks to agents while simultaneously determining safe, time-efficient trajectories through the environment. When tasks are dynamic, such as rendezvous objectives, allocation decisions depend not only on which agent is best suited for a task, but also on when and where that task can be reached. This paper presents a solution to this problem, which combines Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS) for trajectory optimization with the Consensus-Based Bundle Algorithm (CBBA) for distributed task allocation. In our approach, GCS finds optimal trajectories through dynamic environments using a time-extended (3D+time) configuration space. At the same time, CBBA coordinates task assignments across agents, enabling informed decision-making in a moving environment. We then connect allocation and planning to allow the agents to avoid collisions in the 3D+time configuration space and provide accurate time estimates for task completion. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in simulated cluttered environments with static and dynamic tasks.

</details>


### [177] [As You Wish: Mission Planning with Formal Verification using LLMs in Precision Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18519)
*Marcos Abel Zuzuárregui,Stefano Carpin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合线性时序逻辑（LTL）和大语言模型（LLM）的反馈式任务规划架构，用于精准农业中的机器人系统，以解决自然语言指令的歧义性问题，并通过双LLM机制减少偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于自然语言存在固有歧义，仅依赖LLM生成的任务计划可能无法准确满足用户需求，因此需要一种能确保任务规范被正确理解和验证的机制。

Method: 在原有基于LLM的使命规划器基础上引入多反馈循环，利用线性时序逻辑（LTL）形式化用户意图，并采用两个不同的商用大语言模型分别负责规范生成与验证，以降低偏见并提升准确性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能有效提升任务规划的准确性和可靠性，验证了集成LTL验证机制在全自主流程中的可行性，同时揭示了当前LLM生成有用LTL公式的能力仍有限。

Conclusion: 通过引入LTL驱动的反馈机制和双LLM验证架构，可显著缓解自然语言歧义带来的风险，为面向复杂应用的自主机器人系统提供了更安全、可信的任务规划方案。

Abstract: Though robotic systems are now being commercialized and deployed in various industries, many of these systems are highly specialized and often require an advanced skill set to operate and ensure they perform as instructed. To mitigate this problem, we recently introduced a mission planner leveraging LLMs to synthesize mission plans in precision agriculture based on mission descriptions provided in natural language. While the system demonstrates impressive performance, it also suffers from the inherent ambiguities of natural language. In this paper, we extend our system to address this issue by introducing multiple feedback loops in the planning architecture that leverage linear temporal logic (LTL) to ensure the mission planning system meets the specifications formulated by the user while still using natural language. To mitigate potential bias, this is achieved by using two different commercial LLMs in charge of the specification and verification subtasks. Through extensive experiments, we highlight the strengths and limitations of integrating mission verification into a fully autonomous pipeline, particularly regarding an LLM's ability to generate valuable LTL formulas, and show how our proposed implementation addresses and solves these challenges.

</details>


### [178] [DREAM-Chunk: Reactive Action Chunking with Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18589)
*Wenxi Chen,Kaidi Zhang,Chi Lin,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yu She,Yuejiang Liu,Raymond A. Yeh,Shaoshuai Mou,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DREAM-Chunk是一种测试时扩展方法，通过引入轻量级潜在世界模型增强基于分块的策略，提升视觉-语言-动作模型在随机动态环境下的鲁棒性，无需额外微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作分块策略在执行过程中因开环控制难以应对动态不确定性、硬件误差和部分可观测性问题，缺乏足够的反应能力。

Method: 提出DREAM-Chunk方法，在测试时生成多个候选动作分块，通过潜在世界模型预测其未来状态，并选择与实际观测最匹配的分块执行，利用测试时计算覆盖多种可能的未来路径。

Result: 在Kinetix基准和四个操作任务中验证了DREAM-Chunk的有效性，结果表明该方法在不同噪声水平、采样规模和VLA策略下均提升了策略的鲁棒性，尤其在演示包含纠正行为时效果更显著。

Conclusion: DREAM-Chunk通过测试时多轨迹预测与匹配机制，有效增强了动作分块策略在复杂环境中的适应性和稳定性，为无需训练的部署优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a common interface for vision-language-action (VLA) models, enabling low-frequency policy inference to drive high-frequency robot execution. However, once an action chunk is committed, its open-loop execution can be brittle under stochastic dynamics, hardware execution errors, and partial observability. We propose DREAM-Chunk, a test-time scaling method that augments chunking-based policies with a lightweight latent world model, without requiring additional policy fine-tuning. At test time, DREAM-Chunk samples multiple candidate action chunks, rolls out their predicted latent futures, and selects actions from the chunk whose predicted state best matches the observed rollout. In this way, DREAM-Chunk uses additional test-time computation to cover multiple plausible stochastic futures and improve reactivity during long-horizon chunk execution. On the Kinetix benchmark, DREAM-Chunk improves robustness under increasing action noise and benefits from larger candidate sample sizes, especially when demonstrations contain corrective behaviors. We further validate DREAM-Chunk on four manipulation tasks across two robot platforms and two VLA policies under various sources of stochasticity. Across simulation and hardware experiments, DREAM-Chunk improves the robustness of action-chunking policies in stochastic dynamics.

</details>


### [179] [Benchmarking Action Spaces in Reinforcement Learning for Vision-based Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18594)
*Seyed Alireza Azimi,Homayoon Farrahi,Abhishek Naik,Colin Bellinger,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估了不同动作空间表示（如关节速度、姿态增量等）在基于视觉的抓取和推动物体任务中的表现，发现关节速度动作空间在平滑性和任务性能方面最优，并为强化学习实践者提供了仿真到现实迁移中选择动作空间的实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界的强化学习应用中，动作空间的选择对运动平滑性、安全性和任务性能有重要影响，但缺乏针对视觉驱动操作任务的动作空间系统性比较研究。

Method: 在两个基于视觉的操作任务（物体抓取和推动）中，对比了姿态增量、姿态速度、关节位置增量和关节速度四种动作空间；通过仿真训练并采用sim-to-real迁移部署到真实世界进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，动作空间表示显著影响sim-to-real的性能表现，其中关节速度动作空间在运动平滑性和最终任务成功率方面表现最佳。

Conclusion: 关节速度是最适合所研究的视觉驱动操作任务的动作空间，研究结果为实际强化学习系统的设计提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: In real-world reinforcement learning (RL), the choice of action space can play a key role in shaping motion smoothness, safety, and overall task performance. In this study, we evaluate pose increment, pose velocity, joint position increment, and joint velocity across two vision-based manipulation tasks: object picking and pushing. We train policies in simulation and deploy them to the real world using sim-to-real transfer. We find that action-space representation indeed significantly affects sim-to-real performance. In particular, we find that the joint velocity action space is best for the vision-based picking and pushing tasks in terms of smoothness and final task performance. We also provide practical guidance for RL practitioners in choosing action spaces for both simulation and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [180] [Admittance-Based Surface Alignment for Human-in-the-Loop Robotic Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18601)
*Antara Banerjee,Colin Acton,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种实时闭环机器人姿态控制框架，用于高精度视觉检测，结合操作者输入与感知驱动的表面对齐，实现了稳定的法线跟踪和0.4°的平均姿态误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在感知噪声和表面不规则的情况下，需要精确对齐末端执行器与局部表面几何，传统离线运动规划无法适应实时人为调整，需具备反应性和顺应性的控制架构。

Method: 设计基于导纳的控制框架，将末端执行器建模为在粘滞介质中运动的虚拟球体，构建具有物理可解释性的质量-阻尼系统，融合操作者指令与姿态误差实现同步顺应运动。

Result: 在6自由度机械臂上验证了该框架，实现了稳定的法线跟踪，最终平均姿态误差为0.4°。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制方法能有效整合人类操作与感知反馈，实现实时、顺应性的高精度视觉检测任务，适用于航空航天、半导体和医疗制造等工业场景。

Abstract: Precision visual inspection underpins quality assurance across aerospace, semiconductor, and medical manufacturing, where undetected surface anomalies on high-value parts translate directly into scrap, rework, and field failures. Robotic visual inspection requires precise alignment between the end-effector and local surface geometry in the presence of perception noise and surface irregularities. In industrial settings, a human operator is often kept in the loop via teleoperation or shared autonomy, introducing real-time adjustments that render purely offline motion planning inadequate. This motivates control architectures capable of reactive, compliant behavior under combined human and perceptual uncertainty. This paper presents a novel real-time, closed-loop robotic orientation control pipeline for precision visual inspection, with an admittance-based framework that unifies operator input and perception-driven surface alignment. We design the end-effector as a virtual sphere moving through a viscous medium, such that the resulting physically interpretable mass--damper system generates synchronized, compliant motion from orientation error and operator commands. We validate the framework on a 6-DOF manipulator demonstrating stable normal-tracking and a final mean orientation error of 0.4°.

</details>


### [181] [SC3-Eval: Evaluating Robot Foundation Models via Self-Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18610)
*Wei-Cheng Tseng,Gashon Hussein,Yuzhu Dong,Allen Z. Ren,Lucy X. Shi,XuDong Wang,Sergey Levine,Zhaoshuo Li,Jinwei Gu,Florian Shkurti,Ming-Yu Liu,Quan Vuong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SC3-Eval是一种基于自一致视频生成的机器人策略评估方法，通过前向-逆向动力学、跨视角和测试时一致性提升仿真准确性，在真实世界任务中实现了高相关性与强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中评估通用机器人操作策略成本高、速度慢且难以扩展，现有视频世界模型在生成长序列时存在误差累积、多视角不一致和分布外泛化问题。

Method: 提出SC3-Eval，利用预训练视频基础模型，引入三种一致性：前向-逆向动力学一致性（联合预测帧与恢复动作）、跨视角一致性（多相机视图互修复）和测试时一致性（用逆动力学作为不确定性信号终止漂移 rollout）。

Result: 在七个真实世界视觉-语言-动作策略上，SC3-Eval实现0.929的闭环皮尔逊相关系数和0.119的MMRV，优于三个强基线，并能复现真实失败模式，支持细粒度诊断。

Conclusion: SC3-Eval通过三重一致性机制有效提升视频世界模型在机器人策略评估中的准确性和鲁棒性，支持可扩展、高保真的仿真评估，并具备跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Evaluating generalist robot manipulation policies in the real world is expensive, slow, and difficult to scale. Action-conditioned video world models offer a scalable alternative by simulating policy rollouts. Autoregressive rollouts accumulate compounding errors, observations across multiple camera views must remain mutually consistent, and the evaluator must generalize to policies whose behaviors lie outside the training distribution. We address these challenges with SC3-Eval, a self-consistent video generation recipe that adapts a pre-trained video foundation model into an accurate policy evaluator by enforcing three complementary forms of consistency. First, forward-inverse dynamics consistency jointly trains the model to predict frames from actions and to recover actions from frames, anchoring generated rollouts to a physically plausible action manifold and counteracting the drift a forward-only model cannot penalize. Second, cross-view consistency trains the model to inpaint each camera view from the other, keeping the multi-camera observation coherent over long rollouts without any explicit memory mechanism. Third, test-time consistency reuses the inverse dynamics mode at inference as a per-action-chunk uncertainty signal that terminates rollouts whose generated frames drift away from the requested actions. We also demonstrate SC3-Eval rollouts reproduce the failure modes that policies exhibit in real-world rollouts, supporting fine-grained diagnostic comparison rather than aggregate ranking alone. Across seven real-world vision-language-action policies, SC3-Eval attains a closed-loop Pearson correlation of $0.929$ and MMRV of $0.119$, outperforming three strong prior video-model-based baselines, and generalizes to new tasks.

</details>


### [182] [SRL: Combining SLIP Model and Reinforcement Learning for Agile Robotic Jumping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18625)
*Xiaowen Hu,Linqi Ye,Yudi Zhu,Chenyue Shao,Rankun Li,Qingdu Li,Yan Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Spring-loaded Reinforcement Learning (SRL)的混合框架，结合SLIP模型的物理基础与强化学习的适应能力，实现更稳定、高效的机器人跳跃控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLIP模型在不规则地形上性能下降，而强化学习需要大量探索数据，因此希望结合两者优势以提升机器人跳跃的稳定性与适应性。

Method: 将基于SLIP的前馈控制信号与强化学习驱动的实时反馈相结合，构建SRL框架，并在多种仿真和真实环境中进行验证。

Result: SRL相比基线方法训练时间更短，跳跃更稳定，位置跟踪误差低于0.1米，速度跟踪误差在±3%以内，并在双足、四足、平地、楼梯及sim-to-real场景中表现出强适应性。

Conclusion: SRL有效融合了物理模型与数据驱动方法的优势，具有在复杂环境中实际部署的潜力。

Abstract: Robotic jumping is pivotal in applications such as search and rescue and logistics, where crossing obstacles and enhancing mobility efficiency are critical. The Spring-Loaded Inverted Pendulum (SLIP) model leverages simplified spring-mass dynamics that naturally encode biologically plausible hopping motions, yet its performance degrades on irregular terrain due to idealized assumptions regarding contact and joint dynamics. Meanwhile, Reinforcement Learning (RL) can adapt to diverse and complex environments but often requires extensive data from unguided exploration. The complementary strengths of SLIP's physically grounded baseline and RL's adaptive capabilities motivate a hybrid framework that overcomes these individual limitations. We therefore propose Spring-loaded Reinforcement Learning (SRL), which integrates SLIP-based feedforward control signals with RL-driven real-time feedback, enabling continuous optimization of robotic jumping. Experimental results demonstrate that SRL can achieve more stable jumps with much less training time than the baseline method, maintaining an average position tracking error below 0.1 m and velocity tracking errors within +/-3% of the target values. Through bipedal and quadrupedal simulations of ground and stair jumping, as well as sim-to-sim and sim-to-real validations, SRL exhibits robust adaptability to various task requirements and environmental complexities, underscoring its potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [183] [Self-Supervised Mask-Aware Transformers for Fault-Tolerant FBG Force Sensing in Minimally Invasive Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18628)
*Peibo Sun,Shiyuan Dong,Shucheng Ye,Jianrong Cai,Yushan Liu,Hongen Liao,Tianqi Huang,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自监督掩码感知Transformer的统一方法，用于解决微创手术机器人中光纤布拉格光栅（FBG）传感器存在的跨轴耦合和通道丢失问题，实现故障下的优雅退化与实时不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微创手术机器人中，FGB传感器虽有潜力，但面临非线性交叉耦合和通道间歇性失效的问题，导致力估计性能严重下降；现有容错方法依赖组合模型库，校准成本高且难以扩展。

Method: 设计一种统一的自监督掩码感知Transformer架构，通过掩码通道重建预训练编码器，并采用清洁与受损视图联合优化目标进行微调，结合动态损坏课程学习；同时引入并行不确定性预测头，通过异方差高斯负对数似然训练实现单次前向传播的置信度输出。

Result: 在8通道FBG数据集上验证，正常情况下RMSE为0.0066 N，在4通道严重失效时退化至0.0126 N，显著优于包含255个模式专用神经网络的模型库（4通道丢失时为0.0154 N），且无需模式特定校准。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了对多通道FBG传感器在复杂故障下的鲁棒力估计，支持动态容错与实时不确定性量化，降低了系统维护成本，具有良好的临床应用前景。

Abstract: In minimally invasive surgical robotics, catheter-scale Fiber Bragg Grating (FBG) sensors are promising due to their ability to estimate multi-dimensional forces by multiplexing several optical channels. However, deploying these compact multi-channel sensors introduces two critical engineering challenges: inherent nonlinear cross-axis coupling during complex deformations, and intermittent channel dropouts caused by fiber fractures in constrained workspaces. These compounding issues severely degrade force estimation. Existing fault-tolerant approaches rely on combinatorial model banks, which scale exponentially with the channel count and demand prohibitively expensive per-pattern calibration. In this paper, we propose a unified, self-supervised mask-aware Transformer that explicitly models channel availability to enable graceful degradation under diverse and dynamic sensor failures. The encoder is pretrained via masked-channel reconstruction on unlabeled data streams and fine-tuned for force regression using a balanced clean-and-corrupted-view objective alongside a dynamic corruption curriculum. Furthermore, a parallel uncertainty head, trained via heteroscedastic Gaussian negative log-likelihood, predicts per-axis confidence in a single forward pass, circumventing the overhead of multi-pass ensembles. Evaluated on a catheter-scale 8-channel FBG dataset, our single unified model achieves a nominal Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 0.0066~N and degrades gracefully to 0.0126~N under severe 4-channel failures. This significantly outperforms a comprehensive model bank of 255 per-pattern neural networks (0.0154~N at 4-channel loss) while eliminating pattern-specific calibration.

</details>


### [184] [DNN Koopman-Based Deviation Compensation for UGV Path Tracking Control on Coupled Slope and Potholed Road](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18630)
*Jian Zhao,Wenbo Zhou,Zhicheng Chen,Bing Zhu,Jiayi Han,Dongjian Song,Yinju Lin,Peixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度神经网络（DNN）Koopman算子的补偿策略，用于提升无人地面车辆（UGV）在复杂非结构化路面下的路径跟踪性能。结合Laguerre模型预测控制与事件触发协同机制，显著提高了跟踪精度和系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于非公路环境中地形复杂多变，传统路径跟踪方法难以应对如坑洼路面和斜坡耦合等扰动，导致UGV跟踪性能下降。因此需要一种更智能、自适应的补偿机制来提升鲁棒性和精度。

Method: 首先采用解耦误差项的自适应遗忘递推最小二乘法估计轮胎侧偏刚度；然后设计基于Laguere函数的模型预测控制（LMPC）策略以降低计算负担；接着结合Koopman算子理论与DNN提出DK偏差补偿方法；最后建立事件触发并行协同（EPC）机制实现LMPC与DK的动态融合。

Result: 硬件在环（HiL）实验表明，所提方法在多种工况下路径跟踪性能提升超过11.5%，有效抑制了坑洼路面扰动的影响，并保证了转向指令可行性与车辆稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将DNN-Koopman偏差补偿与LMPC结合，通过EPC机制实现了高精度、低计算成本且稳定的UGV路径跟踪，适用于复杂非结构化地形环境。

Abstract: Unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) operating in off-road scenarios are confronted with complex terrain disturbances that can substantially degrade path tracking performance. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a deep neural network (DNN) Koopman-based deviation compensation strategy for UGV path tracking control. Firstly, based on the vehicle dynamic function on coupled slope, an adaptive forgetting recursive least squares method with decoupled error terms is designed to estimate tire cornering stiffness. On this basis, a Laguerre model predictive control (LMPC) path tracking control strategy is designed by incorporating Laguerre functions, which can reduce computational resource usage while maintaining reliable tracking performance across different coupled slope scenarios. Then, by integrating Koopman operator theory with DNN, a DNN Koopman (DK) path deviation compensation method is proposed, which significantly improves the path tracking accuracy of UGV under potholed road disturbances. Furthermore, an event-triggered parallel cooperative (EPC) compensation mechanism that couples LMPC with DK is established based on compensation activation criteria and credibility verification. This mechanism improves path tracking accuracy on potholed road while ensuring the feasibility of overall steering command and stability of vehicle after DK compensation. Finally, a hardware-in-the-loop (HiL) experimental platform is constructed for validation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed UGV path tracking strategy improves tracking performance by more than 11.5% across multiple operating conditions.

</details>


### [185] [ROBOSHACKLES: A Safety Dataset for Human-Injury Prevention in Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18632)
*Zhuowen Yin,Chongyang Liu,Wenzhang Yang,Renjue Li,Yinxing Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于防止机器人对人类造成伤害的安全关键数据构建管道，并构建了包含10,000个视频片段的数据集ROBOSHACKLES，实验表明现有具身基础模型在安全关键场景中均会产生不安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于无法安全或合乎伦理地收集机器人伤害人类或制造危险家庭情况的真实数据，具身基础模型（EFMs）在防止人身伤害方面的安全对齐研究仍然不足。

Method: 从真实的DROID观测出发，通过场景理解、具有危害意识的图像编辑、时序提示生成和单次rollout合成来构建数据集；使用Wan2.7从编辑后的危险状态合成逼真的机器人动作序列。

Result: 构建了包含10,000个视频片段的数据集ROBOSHACKLES，涵盖两类直接伤害和四类间接伤害；评估发现所有测试的代表性EFMs模型在安全关键场景中都会生成不安全动作，不安全动作生成率达到100%。

Conclusion: ROBOSHACKLES可作为可扩展的基准和训练资源，用于机器人执行动作前的拒绝学习和危险预判研究。

Abstract: Embodied Foundation Models (EFMs) integrate multimodal understanding, future-state reasoning, and executable robot actions. Yet their safety alignment for human-injury prevention remains underexplored, primarily because real-world data of robots harming humans or creating hazardous household situations cannot be safely or ethically collected. To address this challenge, we propose a safety-critical data construction pipeline for human-injury prevention in EFMs.Starting from real DROID observations, our construction pipeline proceeds through scene understanding, hazard-aware image editing, temporal prompt generation, and single-pass rollout synthesis. The temporal prompts specify the expected scene evolution, while Wan2.7 synthesizes realistic robotic rollouts from the edited hazardous states in a single pass. Using this pipeline, we construct ROBOSHACKLES, a 10,000-clip robotic video dataset derived from real DROID observations, spanning two direct-harm and four indirect-harm categories. To ensure dataset quality, we assess task completion and visual quality with automatic metrics, and evaluate six representative EFMs under a refusal-based safety criterion. Results show that all evaluated models produce unsafe actions in the tested safety-critical scenarios, yielding a 100% unsafe action generation rate.
